Motivation: 理解神经网络训练中两种看似矛盾的现象——阶梯式成本下降与平滑幂律损失——并解释为何在微观结构迥异的架构（感知机、注意力层、混合专家、卷积）中都能出现这类行为，从而识别出决定学习的少数几个相关集体变量。

Method: 利用对称性分析：网络层是若干可互换单元的求和，故重标号单元不变；结合平滑性与单位梯度在原点为零的条件，对称性强制唯一的普适展开形式（二次项 Tr[WW^T A(x)]），将架构特异性全部封入结构矩阵 A(x)。训练动力学在 M=WW^T 上闭合，当数据矩阵共享本征基时简化为 Lotka-Volterra 方程，分析其模式开启的时序结构及奇异极限行为。

Result: 理论预测：初始权重越小，模式开启时间间隔越大，平台期作为光滑流的奇异极限出现；当大量模式未分辨时，事件合并为训练时间上的幂律，其指数由理论决定。数值实验在多种训练方法和架构上均验证了该预测。

Conclusion: 对称性将看似不同的架构统一为同一模型（不同 A(x) 对应各架构），揭示阶梯与幂律两种学习行为是同一起源于模式依序开启的共同现象，为理解神经网络训练动力学提供了普适的理论框架。

Abstract: Neural networks trained by gradient descent on a smooth cost function can nevertheless learn in steps: the cost holds on long plateaus and then drops abruptly. Meanwhile, training losses instead follow smooth power laws. Variants of both behaviors occur in architectures with very different microscopic structures, which is the signature of a few relevant collective variables. We show that a symmetry fixes what those variables are: a network layer is a sum over interchangeable units, so relabeling the units leaves it unchanged; given smoothness and the condition that a unit's gradient vanish at the origin, symmetry then enforces a universal leading form for the expansion about the near-zero weights present at the start of training, the quadratic $\Tr[WW^{\top}A(x)]$, in which every architectural detail is confined to a single ``structure matrix" $A(x)$ that we compute for each architecture. Perceptrons, attention layers, mixtures of experts, and convolutions become one model at different $A$. Its training dynamics then close on the ``order parameter" $M=WW^{\top}$ and, whenever the data matrices share an eigenbasis, reduce to a Lotka--Volterra equation whose modes switch on one after another. The smaller the initial weights, the further apart the switch-on times, and the plateaus appear as a singular limit of a smooth flow; when many modes are unresolved the same events merge into a power law in training time whose exponent the theory predicts. We confirm both numerically across training methods and architectures.

</details>


### [239] [Where You Measure Decides What You Measure: Position Selection in Ablation-Based SAE Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13337)
*Valentin Noël*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了稀疏自编码器（SAE）评估中的一个关键缺陷：在测量潜在特征（latent）的因果效应时，默认在激活最强的token处测量，但"最强激活token"的位置由字典本身决定，受训练拟合选择影响很大。即使两个字典对同一潜在特征编码几乎相同，也会选择不同token进行测量。作者发现这一位置差异贡献了比较结果的绝大部分方差（7.6%-11.9%），且随语料规模增大而恶化。他们提出一个一行代码的修正协议，并提供可比较的基于消融的因果指标，以及五项已发表论文的审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE用于解释语言模型内部计算，常规验证潜在特征重要性的方法是在其激活最强处关闭它并观察变化。但"最强激活位置"并非中性选择——它由被评估的字典决定，而非实验者决定。更换字典后测量位置会移动，导致两个字典的比较实质上是"在不同位置"比较，使结论失真。

Method: 作者使用Google发布的两个SAE，通过解码器相似性匹配潜在特征对，分析匹配对中选择的不同token比例。随后从同一初始化训练六个SAE（仅拟合选择不同），确保潜在特征语义相同，定量分解比较方差中"字典差异"与"位置差异"的贡献。还跨越16倍语料规模范围测试位置一致性，并提出标准化协议：在报告中必须注明测量位置，提供基于消融的因果数字。

Result: 即使两个字典对潜在特征编码几乎相同，也有很大比例选择不同测量token。在六个同语义字典的比较中，7.6%-11.9%的方差被误判为"字典对潜在特征的分歧"，实为位置差异，一旦统一测量位置即降至接近零。增加评估数据量（16倍范围）反而使位置一致性更差，问题随规模增大而恶化。

Conclusion: SAE评估中未注明测量位置的因果数字，描述的不仅是潜在特征本身，还包含了测量位置的信息。必须报告测量token才能使跨论文的结果可比，修正只需一行代码。这一发现对SAE评估协议的可复现性和严谨性具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders are meant to name the things a language model computes, and the usual way to check that a latent matters is to switch it off and see what changes. But a latent fires at many tokens, and the effect has to be measured at one of them. The convention is to measure where the latent fires hardest. That choice is almost never reported, and it is not made by the experimenter: it is made by the dictionary under evaluation. Change the dictionary and the measurement moves to a different token. We show this is not a detail. Take two sparse autoencoders released by Google for the same model and match their latents by decoder similarity: even among the pairs the two dictionaries encode almost identically, they pick different tokens for a large share of them. Two dictionaries compared under the usual protocol are therefore very often compared at different places. To separate the convention from the dictionaries we train six autoencoders from one initialisation, differing only in fitting choices, so that a latent means the same thing in each. Most of the variance such a comparison reads as "these dictionaries disagree about this latent" turns out to be the position instead: it falls from 7.6% and 11.9% of variance to near zero once every dictionary is measured at the same token. More evaluation data does not rescue it. Across a sixteenfold range of corpus sizes the dictionaries agree less about where to measure, not more, so the problem grows with scale. The correction is one line of evaluation code. We give the protocol an ablation-based causal number must report to be comparable across papers, and an audit of five published papers against it. In short: a causal number reported without its position describes the token it was taken at as much as the latent it was taken from.

</details>


### [240] [Simulation-to-real transfer learning for infrared spectroscopic chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13341)
*Yusen Tan,Yixuan Chen,Zheng Fang,Pan Liu,Yifan Li,Qinyu Guo,Zhedong Lin,Yuqiang Li,Xiangxiang Zeng,Tong Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UltraIR是一个拥有超过1亿参数的红外光谱基础模型，通过在约6000万条模拟红外光谱上进行预训练（包括光谱重建、分子指纹相似度对齐和官能团预测三个任务），实现从模拟到真实的迁移学习，在官能团预测、分子结构解析、理化性质预测、混合物组分识别与定量、细菌分类、药用植物产地溯源、微塑料分类和土壤性质预测等多个下游任务中均优于传统机器学习和任务特定深度学习方法，且在少量标注数据和零样本跨仪器/跨实验室场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统红外光谱分析依赖人工经验和参考谱库，劳动密集且难以规模化；现有机器学习方法多为任务或数据特定，需要大量标注数据，且跨分析目标与实验数据集的迁移能力差。因此需要一种通用的、可迁移的基础模型来解决红外光谱化学信息的可靠提取问题。

Method: UltraIR采用基础模型范式：在约6000万个模拟红外光谱上通过三个预训练目标（光谱重建、分子指纹相似度对齐、官能团预测）进行预训练，然后针对具体下游分析目标使用任务特定标签或目标进行微调适配，实现从模拟光谱到真实实验光谱的迁移学习。

Result: 在官能团预测、分子结构解析、理化性质预测、混合物组分识别与定量、细菌分类、药用植物产地溯源与成分定量、微塑料分类和土壤性质预测等九类任务上，UltraIR均优于传统机器学习基线和任务特定深度学习基线；在有限标注实验光谱下表现强劲，并能在不同傅里叶变换红外光谱仪和实验室之间实现同一分析任务的零样本推理。

Conclusion: UltraIR证明了大规模模拟数据预训练结合迁移学习是构建红外光谱通用分析能力的有效路径，为复杂真实世界样本的化学传感提供了高适应性、数据高效的基础模型方案。

Abstract: Infrared (IR) spectroscopy is widely used for chemical sensing, but extracting reliable chemical information from spectra remains challenging. Conventional interpretation is labor-intensive, relies on prior knowledge and reference spectra, and is difficult to scale, whereas most machine-learning methods are tailored to individual tasks or datasets, require large labeled training sets, and transfer poorly across analytical objectives and experimental datasets. Here we introduce UltraIR, a foundation model for IR spectroscopy with more than 100 million parameters that enables simulation-to-real transfer learning for chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples. UltraIR is pretrained on approximately 60 million simulated IR spectra using spectral reconstruction, molecular fingerprint similarity alignment, and functional-group prediction, then adapted to downstream objectives with task-specific labels or targets. Across functional-group prediction, molecular structure elucidation, physicochemical property prediction, mixture-component identification and quantification, bacterial classification, medicinal-herb geographic origin traceability and constituent quantification, microplastics classification, and soil property prediction, UltraIR outperforms conventional machine-learning and task-specific deep-learning baselines. It performs strongly with limited labeled experimental spectra and in zero-shot inference for the same analytical task across Fourier-transform infrared spectrometers and laboratories, providing a route to adaptable, data-efficient chemical sensing from complex real-world samples.

</details>


### [241] [When Local Variance Optimality Is Not Enough: RoPE-Aligned Q/K Rotations for Dynamic 4-Bit Quantisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13365)
*Shuhan Wang,Yilin Luo,Nan Xu,Chi Wang Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于旋转的后训练量化中，与RoPE可交换的逐对（两通道）旋转变换相比全头Hadamard变换的效果。结果表明，在W4A4KV4动态量化设置下，逐对旋转未能提升精度，且在全检查点上均劣于全头混合；与Hadamard组合后可满足选定的困惑度区间标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE将注意力头划分为二维频率对，这引出一个核心问题：在尊重这种分解的前提下，能否构造比全头混合更好的正交变换来减少离群值引起的量化误差？此前工作已建立与RoPE可交换的逐对旋转，但缺乏对其在动态量化场景下有效性的系统评估与理论分析。

Method: （1）理论上证明在频率互异条件下，没有任何其他单头正交映射与RoPE可交换（反命题结论）；（2）在共享头参数化下，推导使较大通道方差最小化的旋转角度（基于池化协方差、位置平均的替代目标），并验证实现达到解析最小值；（3）在四个检查点上进行动态W4A4KV4量化实验，对比逐对旋转、全头Hadamard及其组合；并沿"两通道到全头混合"的受控插值探究混合支持度的影响。

Result: 共享头的逐对配置在所有检查点上均未提升精度，反而增加了短、长上下文下的困惑度；将逐对旋转与Hadamard组合后满足±0.05-PPL区间判据。仅从K估计共享角度可改善逐对配置，但未能缩小与全头混合的差距。沿受控插值，随着混合支持度增加，K范围、相对量化误差和困惑度退化均降低。

Conclusion: 结构化代理目标的最优性并不保证降低量化误差，因为代理目标（位置平均的池化协方差二阶矩）与量化器的尺度设定统计量（逐token群组范围）不一致，且逐对变换仅有两通道混合支持度。两个对齐问题共同导致逐对旋转在动态W4A4KV4设置下劣于全头混合。

Abstract: Rotation-based post-training quantisation commonly applies an orthogonal transform across an entire attention head to reduce outlier-induced error. RoPE instead partitions each head into two-dimensional frequency pairs, raising the question of whether a transform respecting this decomposition can improve on full-head mixing. Prior work has established the per-pair rotations that commute with RoPE. We state the converse result that, for distinct frequencies, no other single-head orthogonal map commutes with RoPE. For the head-shared parameterisation used in our experiments, we then derive the rotation angle that minimises the larger channel variance under a pooled-covariance, position-averaged surrogate and verify that the implementation attains its analytic minimum. The evaluated head-shared pairwise configuration does not improve accuracy in the tested dynamic W4A4KV4 setting. Across four checkpoints, replacing the full-head Hadamard with this configuration increases perplexity at both short and long context lengths. Composing the pairwise rotation with the Hadamard satisfies the selected $\pm0.05$-PPL interval criterion under the default estimator. Estimating the shared angle from K alone improves pairwise-only on every checkpoint but does not close its gap to full-head mixing. The analytic objective controls a position-averaged second moment of a pooled calibration covariance, whereas the dynamic quantiser sets its step from a tokenwise group range. The pairwise transform also has only two-channel mixing support. Along a controlled interpolation from two-channel to full-head mixing, K range, relative quantisation error, and perplexity degradation decrease as support increases. These results show that optimality for a structured surrogate need not reduce quantisation error when the surrogate and mixing support are misaligned with the quantiser's scale-setting statistic.

</details>


### [242] [Reduced Matrix Multiplication: Input-Adaptive Matrix-Product Reduction for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13426)
*Zixuan Lan,Yanhong Li,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练、输入自适应的推理加速方法RMM（Reduced Matrix Multiplication），通过在收缩维度上选择信息丰富的切片来减少Transformer矩阵乘法计算量，在1B到70B参数的多种语言模型上验证了其精度-效率的可控权衡，并扩展到多模态场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的语言模型虽然性能强大，但推理成本高，主要源于反复进行的高维矩阵乘法运算。现有方法需要在训练阶段修改权重或引入额外开销，缺乏一种简单、训练自由且可动态适应输入的推理优化手段。

Method: RMM是一种无需训练、输入自适应的推理方法。它沿矩阵乘法的收缩维度选择信息量最丰富的切片进行运算，不修改任何模型权重。通过简单的保留率（retention ratio）控制参数，实现平滑且可预测的精度-效率权衡。在多种任务（判别式、自回归生成、长上下文）和从1B到70B的多种模型规模上进行了系统评估，并通过机制性消融实验分析了注意力侧与MLP组件的可缩减性差异。最后使用自定义kernel在NVIDIA A100上进行墙钟时间基准测试。

Result: 缩减容忍度因模型家族、任务、组件和保留率而异，但通常随模型规模增大而提升。在中等缩减程度下，RMM在判别式、自回归生成和长上下文设置中保持鲁棒性。机制性消融揭示了Transformer内部存在结构性不对称：注意力侧计算远比MLP组件更可缩减。A100上的墙钟基准显示计算节省可转化为实际运行时间收益，尤其在更长序列长度下。该方法同样适用于多模态视觉-语言推理。

Conclusion: RMM为输入自适应的推理时优化提供了一个可扩展的方向，在不修改权重的前提下通过动态选择关键计算切片实现有效加速，并展示了跨模型规模、任务类型和多模态场景的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models achieve strong performance but incur substantial inference cost due to repeated high-dimensional matrix multiplications. We propose Reduced Matrix Multiplication (RMM), a training-free, input-adaptive inference method that reduces Transformer matrix products by selecting informative slices along their contraction dimensions, without modifying model weights. Under a simple retention-ratio control, RMM provides a smooth and predictable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Across language models ranging from 1B to 70B parameters, we find that reduction tolerance depends on the model family, task, component, and retention ratio, although it often improves with model scale. Under moderate reduction, RMM remains robust across the evaluated discriminative, autoregressive generation, and long-context settings. We further show that the same principle extends to multimodal vision-language inference. Mechanistic ablations reveal a structural asymmetry within Transformers: attention-side computations are substantially more reducible than MLP components. Finally, wall-clock benchmarks with custom kernels on an NVIDIA A100 show that these computational savings can translate into practical runtime gains, especially at longer sequence lengths. Together, these results position RMM as a scalable direction for input-adaptive inference-time optimization.

</details>


### [243] [Symmetry-Breaking De Novo Crystal Generation via Markovian Jump Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13457)
*Van Khoa Nguyen,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对称性破缺的晶体扩散生成框架（SbCD），通过马尔可夫跳跃扩散过程模拟从最低对称性先验反向生成完整晶体结构规格，从而显式建模空间群之间的转移，在MP20和MPTS-52数据集上显著优于对称性保持的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的晶体生成模型只能生成到位点对称性的结构，并依赖从经验分布中采样空间群，无法捕获全局对称性和结构依赖性。受物理学中自发对称性破缺现象的启发，作者希望设计一种能够生成完整晶体学规格、同时显式建模空间群之间转移的生成框架。

Method: 提出了Symmetry-breaking Crystal Diffusion（SbCD）框架，其核心创新在于：(1) 从最低对称性先验出发，通过反向生成过程恢复完整晶体结构规格；(2) 利用马尔可夫跳跃扩散过程（Markovian jump-diffusion process）建模对称性破缺动力学，从而在多个空间群之间进行物理上有意义的遍历和转移；(3) 将空间群间的转移显式融入生成过程，提供原则性的方法。

Result: 在MP20和MPTS-52数据集上的从头生成实验中，SbCD相比对称性保持的对应方法取得了大幅性能提升，验证了显式引入空间群间转换的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为晶体材料生成建模提供了新的视角，证明将物理上的对称性破缺原理融入扩散模型可以有效提升晶体结构生成的完整性和质量，为后续晶体生成研究提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Generating crystals has recently attracted significant interest due to their broad applications in materials science. However, existing generative models struggle to produce complete crystallographic specifications, limiting their ability to capture global symmetry and structural dependencies. In particular, current state-of-the-art approaches generate crystals only up to site symmetries and rely on sampling space groups from empirical distributions during generation. Inspired by \emph{spontaneous symmetry breaking} in physics, where crystals break symmetries under external conditions, we propose a novel diffusion-based framework that generates full structure specifications by reversing from the lowest-symmetry priors. Our method leverages a Markovian jump-diffusion process to model these symmetry-breaking dynamics, enabling it to traverse different space groups in a physically motivated manner. Our model, dubbed \emph{Symmetry-breaking Crystal Diffusion} (SbCD), introduces a principled approach to explicitly incorporate inter-space-group transitions into the generative process. In de novo generation experiments on MP20 and MPTS-52, SbCD outperforms its symmetry-preserving counterpart by a substantial margin, offering a promising perspective for generative modeling of crystalline materials.

</details>


### [244] [Doubly Robust Estimation of Causal Effect on CVR with Targeted Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13461)
*Jiayi Dan,Bo Li,Lu Deng,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从半参数理论角度重新审视点击后转化率（CVR）的因果效应估计问题，提出了一种针对链式结构结果的新型双重稳健因果效应估计器，并通过目标正则化框架提升数值稳定性和实际可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVR是电商和广告场景中的关键指标，估计其因果效应具有重要实际意义。然而，现有方法存在两个核心问题：(1) 直接对点击样本应用因果推断方法会产生样本选择偏差和方差增大；(2) 近年提出的"理想损失"方法虽然优化无偏损失，但无法保证损失的无偏性会转化为最终估计器的无偏性。

Method: 从半参数理论出发，开发了一种针对链式结构结果（如CVR）的新型双重稳健因果效应估计器，并详细推导其理论性质。该估计器相比nuisance参数估计实现更快的收敛速度，因而在使用灵活的非参数估计器（包括神经网络）时更加稳健。基于理论发现，进一步设计了基于目标正则化（targeted regularization）的框架来改善数值稳定性和实际适用性。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的大量实验验证了方法的有效性和稳健性。实验还发现，将损失去偏（loss debiasing）与标准因果估计器简单组合的效果不如所提出的方法，突显了针对CVR目标开发具有坚实理论保证的新估计器的必要性。

Conclusion: 针对CVR的因果效应估计需要专门设计具有理论保证的新估计器，而非简单组合已有的损失去偏方法和标准因果推断工具；所提出的双重稳健估计器在理论性质和实际性能上均优于现有方案。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (CVR) is a key metric in various scenarios including e-commerce and advertising, reflecting the efficiency and user experience in the second stage of the conversion process. Estimating the causal effect on CVR is therefore of great practical importance. However, directly applying existing causal inference methods to clicked samples introduces sample selection bias and increased variance due to the exclusion of non-click data. Recent studies on CVR prediction introduce "ideal loss", which optimizes model parameters using an unbiased estimate of the loss over the full sample. Nevertheless, there is no guarantee that unbiasedness of the loss implies unbiasedness of the final estimator.
  We revisit this challenge from the perspective of semiparametric theory. Specifically, we develop a new doubly robust causal effect estimator for chain-structured outcomes such as CVR, and derive its theoretical properties in detail. It achieves a faster convergence rate compared to nuisance parameters estimation and is therefore more robust when using flexible nonparametric estimators, including neural networks. Based on these theoretical findings, we further design a framework based on targeted regularization to improve numerical stability and practical applicability.
  Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world data demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our method. In addition, we find that naively combining loss debiasing with standard causal estimators underperforms our method, highlighting the necessity of developing the new estimator tailored to this CVR-style objective with solid theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [245] [Concept Drift Detection and Adaptive Retraining of Malware Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13465)
*Christofer Washington Berruz Chungata,Martin Jurecek,Katerina Potika,William B. Andreopoulos,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了恶意软件分类模型中概念漂移的自动检测问题，提出了一种基于OCSVM的新方法，并与MK-Means和MMD两种已有技术进行了对比。在四种学习模型（MLP、RF、SVM、XGBoost）和三种场景（静态、周期性、漂移感知）下进行实验，发现所有漂移检测技术都能在保持与周期性重训相当的分类精度的同时大幅提升训练效率，其中OCSVM方法整体表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件检测模型因攻击者持续修改恶意软件而面临概念漂移问题，导致模型性能随时间退化。现有的周期性重训策略计算开销大，而准确的自动概念漂移检测可以在必要时才触发重训，从而兼顾精度与效率。

Method: 提出基于One-Class Support Vector Machines（OCSVM）的自动概念漂移检测方法，并与Minibatch K-Means（MK-Means）和Maximum Mean Discrepancy（MMD）两种技术进行对比。在四种学习模型（MLP、RF、SVM、XGBoost）上比较三种场景：静态（不重训）、周期性（始终重训）、漂移感知（仅在检测到漂移时重训），并使用Pareto Front分析精度与训练效率间的权衡。

Result: 三种概念漂移检测技术在分类精度上均接近周期性重训，但在需要重训的模型数量上大幅减少。基于OCSVM的漂移感知重训总体上优于MK-Means和MMD方法。

Conclusion: 实验结果强有力地证明，可以准确检测恶意软件分类模型中的概念漂移，从而在保持高分类精度的同时显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: Concept drift refers to changes over time in the statistical properties of data, as compared to the data that was used to train a learning model. Machine learning models for malware detection or classification are particularly susceptible to performance degradation caused by concept drift, as attackers constantly modify existing malware. In this chapter, we analyze two machine learning-based approaches to automated concept drift detection-a novel approach based on One-Class Support Vector Machines (OCSVM) and a previously-studied technique based on Minibatch K-Means (MK-Means). For comparison we also consider Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), a statistical technique for detecting changes in multidimensional data. We conduct an extensive series of experiments comparing the effectiveness of four learning models, namely, Multilayer Perceptron, Random Forest, Support Vector Machines, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting. For each of these models, we consider three distinct scenarios: A static scenario where no model retraining occurs, a periodic scenario where models are constantly retrained irrespective of concept drift, and a drift-aware scenario where models are only retrained when concept drift is detected. Under the drift-aware scenario, we analyze the tradeoff between accuracy and training efficiency using Pareto Front analysis. We find that all three concept drift detection techniques achieve classification accuracy comparable to periodic retraining, while offering substantially greater efficiency in terms of the number of models that must be retrained. In addition, drift-aware retraining based on our OCSVM technique generally outperforms the MK-Means and MMD approaches. Overall, these results provide strong evidence that we can accurately detect concept drift in malware classification models.

</details>


### [246] [Active-Trace Complexity Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13467)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Moreau-Yosida未调整Langevin算法(MYULA)用于非光滑复合目标分布采样。作者发现MYULA的离散步误差由参考活性迹B_ref控制而非全局曲率界d/λ，并在结构化的分段线性、Lasso、分组和全变差惩罚下使B_ref独立于λ，将精度依赖从Õ(ε^{-3})提升到Õ(ε^{-2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非光滑复合目标(强凸f+凸Lipschitz g)上进行Langevin采样时，标准未调整Langevin算法无法直接处理非光滑项。MYULA通过Moreau包络平滑非光滑项，但现有误差分析依赖于全局曲率界d/λ，该界随平滑参数λ发散，导致次优的ε^{-3}复杂度。作者希望利用目标结构的稀疏性/分段线性特性获得更紧的曲率-管估计，从而改善复杂度。

Method: 定义g_λ为g的Moreau包络，π_λ为对应平滑目标，a_λ=tr H_λ为H_λ的迹（H_λ是g_λ的a.e./弱Hessian）。关键引入参考活性迹B_ref，即从π_λ出发进行一步MYULA热子步时a_λ的平均值。与全局上界d/λ不同，B_ref刻画了实际访问区域的曲率强度。作者证明MYULA主离散步误差由B_ref控制，并建立了Moreau偏差界√m W_2(π_λ,π) ≤ G²λ/4。对分段线性、Lasso型、分组和全变差惩罚，利用曲率-管估计证明B_ref与λ无关。

Result: 得到迭代复杂度N ≲ (1/m)[L_f + (τ_f+G²+B_ref)/ε²_alg + M_λ/ε_alg]，其中B_ref替代了全局曲率界d/λ。选择λ≍ε/G²时给出针对原始π的端到端保证。通用估计B_ref≤d/λ给出Õ(ε^{-3})精度依赖；对结构化惩罚（分段线性、Lasso、分组、全变差），B_ref独立于λ，得到Õ(ε^{-2})复杂度，且使用相同的经典MYULA核。

Conclusion: 通过引入参考活性迹B_ref替代全局曲率界，作者揭示了MYULA的实际离散误差依赖于算法运行时访问区域的局部曲率特性。对于具有稀疏/分段线性结构的常见惩罚项，可获得与平滑目标相媲美的Õ(ε^{-2})复杂度，无需修改算法本身。这一分析框架为理解和改进非光滑Langevin采样提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study the Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA) for the nonsmooth composite target \[ π(dx)\propto \exp\{-f(x)-g(x)\}\,dx, \qquad x\in\mathbb R^d, \] where \(f\) is \(m\)-strongly convex with \(L_f\)-Lipschitz gradient and \(g\) is convex and \(G\)-Lipschitz. Let \(g_λ\) be the Moreau envelope of \(g\), \(π_λ\) the corresponding smoothed target, and \(a_λ=\operatorname{tr}H_λ\), where \(H_λ\) is the a.e./weak Hessian of \(g_λ\). We show that the leading MYULA discretization error is controlled by the reference active trace \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\), the average of \(a_λ\) along the heat substep of one MYULA update started from \(π_λ\), rather than by the global curvature bound \(d/λ\). If \(M_λ\) is an a.e. upper bound for \(a_λ\), then, up to logarithmic factors, \[ N \lesssim \frac{1}{m} \left[ L_f + \frac{ τ_f+G^2+B_{\mathrm{ref}} }{ \varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}^2 } + \frac{M_λ}{\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}} \right], \qquad τ_f:= \sup_x\operatorname{tr}\nabla^2 f(x), \] iterations suffice to ensure \(\sqrt m\,W_2(μ_N,π_λ)\leq\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}\), where \(μ_N\) is the law of the \(N\)-th iterate and \(W_2\) is the quadratic Wasserstein distance. We also prove the Moreau-bias bound \[ \sqrt m\,W_2(π_λ,π) \leq \frac{G^2λ}{4}. \] Thus, choosing \(λ\asymp\varepsilon/G^2\) gives an end-to-end guarantee for \(π\). The universal estimate \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\leq d/λ\) yields \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})\) accuracy dependence. For the structured piecewise-linear, lasso-type, group, and total-variation penalties considered here, curvature--tube estimates make \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\) independent of \(λ\), yielding \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-2})\) for the same classical MYULA kernel.

</details>


### [247] [Synthetic Persona Pretraining: Alignment from Token Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13482)
*Julian Minder,Viktor Moskvoretskii,Raghav Singhal,Difan Jiao,Andy Arditi,Shaobo Cui,Yiderigun Borjigin,Kartik Bali,Stefan Krsteski,Harsh Raj,Huu Nguyen,Jannik Brinkmann,Ashton Anderson,Roland Aydin,Robert West*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"合成人格预训练"（SPP）范式，在预训练第一步即从零token开始植入期望的助手人格，通过在预训练文档中标注符合价值宪法的第一人称反思文本进行训练，并在后期通过对话数据绑定助手身份。在3B参数、500B token的预训练规模下，SPP在宪法遵循、越狱鲁棒性和道德困境中的错位率上均优于传统对齐方法，且不影响基础能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于语言模型的AI在自主场景中部署日益增多，对齐变得至关重要。但传统方法在对齐阶段才引入价值观和助手身份，此时行为先验已经建立，导致价值观只是"薄薄一层覆盖物"而非根深蒂固，容易引发后续的错位。作者希望探索一种新范式，从预训练的第一步就植入期望人格，使价值观扎根于模型内部。

Method: SPP包含三个步骤：①对预训练文档进行价值对齐的第一人称反思标注（基于规范性价值宪法生成）；②使用标准交叉熵损失在原始预训练文档及其反思文本上预训练，从而在众多人格中植入期望人格；③在用户-助手对话数据上后训练，将期望人格绑定到助手身份上，称为"人格绑定"（persona binding）。

Result: 在3B参数、500B token的预训练规模下，SPP改善了宪法遵循性和越狱鲁棒性，降低了分布外道德困境中的错位率，同时保持了基础能力。干预时机至关重要：与从token零即进行对齐相比，仅在预训练结束时引入SPP会导致宪法遵循更弱、不改变价值优先级、在困境中选择更不对齐。该优势依赖于人格绑定，且随预训练预算增加而增强。

Conclusion: 早期塑造价值观对对齐至关重要，预训练阶段的人格干预是有效的对齐手段。SPP证明将人格植入从token 0开始比后期附加更稳固，且这种优势随计算预算增长而扩大。

Abstract: As language-model-based AI is increasingly deployed in autonomous settings, aligning its goals and values with those of humans becomes critical. Today, alignment, and the assistant identity itself, are typically introduced only after pretraining, once behavioral priors are already established. This can make values a thin overlay, rather than deeply rooted, and facilitate subsequent misalignment. Pursuing a different paradigm, we introduce Synthetic Persona Pretraining (SPP), which installs the desired assistant persona from token zero in pretraining. First, we annotate pretraining documents with value-aligned first-person reflections derived from a normative value constitution. Second, we pretrain via the standard cross-entropy loss on standard pretraining documents as well as their reflections, which installs the desired persona among a multitude of other personas. Finally, we post-train on user-assistant dialogue data, which binds this desired persona to the assistant identity, a process we call persona binding. By pretraining models up to 3B parameters on 500B tokens, we show that SPP improves constitution following and jailbreak robustness, and reduces the misalignment rate in out-of-distribution moral dilemmas, while preserving capabilities. Early intervention matters: compared with alignment from token zero, introducing SPP only at the end of pretraining yields weaker constitution adherence, does not shift value priorities, and leads to less aligned choices in dilemmas. This advantage depends on persona binding and, importantly, increases with pretraining budget. Overall, our results show that shaping values early is critical for alignment and establish pretraining-time persona interventions as an effective approach to do so.

</details>


### [248] [Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique: A Spectral Framework for Equation Discovery, Approximation, and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13504)
*Sabin Roman,Ljupco Todorovski,Saso Dzeroski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique (SORT)，一种稀疏谱框架，通过 L1 正则化回归直接从噪声和不规则采样数据中学习正交基展开系数，主要用于数据驱动的 ODE 发现，可部分替代符号回归和 SINDy 类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的库基稀疏回归方法（如 SINDy）依赖于有限候选函数库对非线性项的精确覆盖，当库缺失关键非线性项时模型会失败；而符号回归等方法在噪声和高维场景下不稳定。需要一个更鲁棒、可直接从观测数据学习的中间表示。

Method: SORT 在正交基上表示向量场，利用 L1 正则化回归直接从观测中估计展开系数，避免显式求积或解析内积计算。学习得到稀疏谱表示后，可进一步指导搜索更简洁的解析形式。

Result: 在动力学系统实验中，当基函数与问题适配时，SORT 匹配或优于库基稀疏回归基线；在稀疏采样、噪声导数估计和表示不匹配情况下表现更稳定。实验还表明低阶主导系数随模型阶数增加而保持，支持一致的模型增长。此外，学习到的展开还支持非线性逼近和高维积分估计。

Conclusion: SORT 提供了一种可复用的中间表示，将系统辨识、逼近和积分统一起来，并将基设计显式纳入科学建模问题中，从而将问题从脆弱的通用项选择转变为领域自适应的基设计。

Abstract: We develop the Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique (SORT), a sparse spectral framework for learning orthonormal-basis expansions from noisy and irregularly sampled data. SORT estimates expansion coefficients directly from observations using L1-regularized regression, avoiding explicit quadrature or analytic inner-product evaluation. The central application is data-driven discovery of ordinary differential equations: vector fields are represented in chosen orthogonal bases and learned as sparse coefficient expansions. This provides a complementary route to symbolic regression, grammar-based discovery, and SINDy-style sparse identification by first recovering a compact spectral representation, which can later guide searches for simpler analytic forms. Across the dynamical-system experiments, SORT matches or improves upon library-based sparse-regression baselines when the basis is well adapted to the problem, and shows more stable degradation under sparse sampling, noisy derivative estimates, and representation mismatch. Specific examples illustrate why this representation is useful: if a finite library misses the problem-specific nonlinearity, the resulting model can fail. SORT is not immune to mismatch, but it shifts the problem away from brittle selection among generic terms to basis design adapted to the problem domain. The experiments also show that dominant low-order coefficients persist as model order increases, supporting order-consistent model growth. Beyond equation discovery, the same learned expansion supports nonlinear approximation and estimation of complex, high-dimensional integrals by coefficient readout. Overall, SORT provides a reusable intermediate representation for system identification, approximation, and integration, while making basis design an explicit part of the scientific modeling problem.

</details>


### [249] [Intern-S2-Preview: Scientific Agentic Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13505)
*Lei Bai,Jiaqi Cao,Chiyu Chen,Guanzhou Chen,Kai Chen,Guangran Cheng,Erfei Cui,Xuanlang Dai,Shengyuan Ding,Shangheng Du,Yanhui Duan,Yue Fan,Youqing Fang,Quan Gan,Yuanyuan Gao,Jiaye Ge,Lixin Gu,Yuzhe Gu,Qipeng Guo,Junjun He,Xin Hong,Ming Hu,Zhouqi Hua,Haian Huang,Junhao Huang,Zixian Huang,Minxi Jin,Lingkai Kong,Alexander Lam,Zehao Li,Zonglin Li,Tianhao Liang,Dahua Lin,Junyao Lin,Tianyang Lin,Zhouhan Lin,Jiangning Liu,Jin Liu,Kuikun Liu,Wenran Liu,Yifei Liu,Yuhong Liu,Yuhong Liu,Zhoumianze Liu,Ziyan Liu,Ziyu Liu,Haijun Lv,Han Lv,Chengqi Lyu,Le Ma,Ningsheng Ma,Zerun Ma,Haoyang Peng,Runyu Peng,Jifei Shan,Zixin Shang,Kou Shi,Xiang Shi,Qisheng Su,Xuerui Su,Hao Sun,Xiao Sun,Yanan Sun,Yu Sun,Huanze Tang,Yinghao Tang,Wenhui Tian,Zhongbo Tian,Bingli Wang,Haomin Wang,Jiarui Wang,Jingzhi Wang,Rui Wang,Xiquan Wang,Yi Wang,Zhecan Wang,Ziyi Wang,Zun Wang,Rubin Wei,Lianyi Wu,Wen Wu,Yue Wu,Yuhan Wu,Zhenyu Wu,Zijian Wu,Shuhao Xing,Jun Xu,Xingle Xu,Xuenan Xu,Xiangchao Yan,Ziang Yan,Bowen Yang,Danni Yang,Lin Yang,Zhiqi Yang,Qian Yao,Haochen Ye,Peng Ye,Jinhui Yin,Jiashuo Yu,Dingbo Yuan,Fei Yuan,Yuhang Zang,Bo Zhang,Chao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Hongjie Zhang,Junming Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Zhuo Zhang,Ziyang Zhang,Haiteng Zhao,Penghao Zhao,Yibo Zhao,Zhonghan Zhao,Zhihang Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Peiheng Zhou,Xin Zhou,Xinyu Zhou,Yunhua Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu,Yicheng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Intern-S2-Preview系列科学智能体基础模型，通过科学多模态预训练、统一后训练流水线（包括SFT、多任务RL、黑箱/白箱智能体RL和同策略蒸馏）构建支持多模态科学理解、推理、生成和长时程任务的模型。397B模型扩展了时间序列建模能力，并引入Memory Decoder作为独立记忆增强路径，在不修改冻结的397B主干的前提下实现快速科学专业化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现越来越需要AI系统能够对多模态科学证据进行推理、与科学工具和环境交互，并在长时间任务跨度内持续取得进展。现有AI模型在这类长时程、多模态、工具交互的科学任务上能力不足。

Method: 训练流水线分两阶段：(1) 科学多模态预训练，涵盖渲染后的科学文档、图文交错数据和多样科学语料；(2) 统一后训练流水线，包含监督微调、可扩展的多任务强化学习、黑箱与白箱智能体RL、同策略蒸馏。技术层面引入了部分rollout与离策略校正、自适应长度正则化、在线推测解码、鲁棒多任务优化、基于轨迹感知的经验组装等训练稳定性与效率优化技术。架构上，Intern-S2-Preview-397B扩展了时间序列建模（从高效长序列理解到数值预测），Memory Decoder作为独立记忆增强路径实现快速科学专业化且不修改冻结的397B主干。

Result: 在科学、多模态、智能体和通用基准上的评估显示，Intern-S2-Preview-397B在多个设置下取得有竞争力或领先的结果。时间序列模块在SciTS上改善了科学信号理解与预测能力；独立的Intern-MemDec-4B扩展将Biology-Instructions平均分从56.92提升到60.32，且无需修改冻结的397B主干。

Conclusion: Intern-S2-Preview系列展示了结合多模态科学预训练与统一后训练流水线（含智能体RL）可有效构建面向长时程科学任务的智能体基础模型；时间序列建模和独立记忆解码器为科学专业化提供了互补且有效的技术路径。

Abstract: Scientific discovery increasingly requires AI systems that can reason over scientific evidence of heterogeneous modalities, interact with scientific tools and environments, and sustain progress across long task horizons. We present Intern-S2-Preview, a series of scientific agentic foundation models designed to support multimodal scientific understanding, reasoning, generation, and long-horizon tasks. The training pipeline begins with scientific multimodal pre-training over rendered scientific documents, interleaved image-text data, and diverse scientific corpora. Starting from the pretrained checkpoint, we apply a unified post-training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning, scalable multi-task reinforcement learning (RL), black- and white-box agentic RL, and on-policy distillation. This pipeline is supported by practical techniques that improve rollout and training stability and efficiency, including partial rollout with off-policy correction, adaptive length regularization, online speculative decoding, robust multi-task optimization, and trace-aware experience assembly for agentic tasks. At the architecture level, Intern-S2-Preview-397B extends time series modelling from efficient long-sequence understanding to numerical forecasting, while Memory Decoder is studied as a separate memory-augmented path for rapid scientific specialization without modifying the frozen 397B backbone. Evaluations across scientific, multimodal, agentic, and general-purpose benchmarks show that Intern-S2-Preview-397B achieves competitive or leading results in multiple settings. The time series modules improve scientific signal understanding and forecasting on SciTS, while the separate Intern-MemDec-4B extension improves the Biology-Instructions average score from 56.92 to 60.32 without modifying the frozen 397B backbone.

</details>


### [250] [Intervention-Aware Clinical World Model for Post-Op Outcome Forecasting in Cardiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13518)
*Yunsung Chung,Yingshuo Liu,Abboud F. Hassan,Han Feng,Mary M. Maleckar,Nassir Marrouche,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种干预感知的临床世界模型，将患者表示为结构化潜在状态，并通过时间排序的术后事件（用药变化、重复干预、生理测量等）逐步演化该状态，用于预测房颤消融术后90天恢复窗口内的长期复发风险。模型在DECAAF-II内部交叉验证中达到AUROC 0.756、AUPRC 0.777，并在无需随访MRI的情况下实现疤痕范围MAE为2.971个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床预测模型将术后结局视为从基线测量到未来端点的单步映射，忽略了恢复过程的时序性和不规则性。实际上术后恢复是一个非均匀时间轨迹：临床观察、用药调整、重复干预和生理测量是异步记录的，且会随时间改变风险评估。需要一个能够动态更新患者状态以反映中期事件影响的模型。

Method: 构建干预感知的临床世界模型：（1）将基线影像编码为3D空间潜在状态；（2）利用手术上下文、静态协变量、经过时间和围事件生理嵌入来更新潜在状态；（3）随访影像仅作为训练阶段的潜在预测监督信号（无需推理时输入）。

Result: 在DECAAF-II重复内部交叉验证中，复发预测AUROC为0.756、AUPRC为0.777；疤痕范围预测MAE为2.971个百分点（推理时无需随访MRI强度）。模型支持不同时间视角的复发风险查询及对空窗期记录的回顾性输入编辑。

Conclusion: 通过学习结构化潜在状态并利用术后90天恢复窗口内的不规则临床记录，模型能够有效提升房颤消融术后长期复发风险的预测能力，且支持灵活的风险查询与输入编辑，展示了临床世界模型在动态病程建模中的潜力。

Abstract: Many clinical prediction models treat post-intervention outcomes as a one-step mapping from baseline measurements to a future endpoint. However, recovery after a procedure often unfolds as an irregular trajectory: clinical observations, medication changes, repeat interventions, and physiological measurements are recorded asynchronously and can change risk assessment over time. We propose an intervention-aware clinical world model that represents each patient with a structured latent state and evolves it through time-ordered post-intervention events. The model first encodes baseline imaging into a 3D spatial latent state. It then updates this state using procedural context, static covariates, elapsed time, and peri-event physiological embeddings. Follow-up imaging provides training-only supervision through a latent forecasting objective. We apply the framework to atrial fibrillation ablation. During the 90-day recovery window, irregular post-procedure records provide clinically meaningful evidence for long-term recurrence risk. In repeated internal cross-validation on DECAAF-II, our model achieves AUROC 0.756 and AUPRC 0.777 for recurrence prediction. It also achieves a scar-extent MAE of 2.971 percentage points without requiring follow-up MRI intensities at inference. The learned state supports recurrence-risk queries at different horizons and retrospective input editing of blanking-period records.

</details>


### [251] [The data geometry of masking diffusion: Certified-optimal schedules via unmasking growth complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13520)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究离散采样的掩码扩散方法，引入了一种名为"解掩码增长复杂度"(UGC)的路径解析数据几何度量。UGC的局部增量直接控制KL离散化误差，统一了Bernoulli子集和固定基数解掩码方案的分析。基于此提出了经过认证的最优采样器，能在高概率下达到预设KL误差，且迭代复杂度在常数因子内接近理论最优过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型的采样质量严重依赖于掩码调度策略，而现有方法缺乏对数据几何结构与离散化误差之间关系的统一理论理解。作者希望建立一种路径解析的度量工具，用以解释和优化不同解掩码方案的性能差异，并指导计算资源的自适应分配。

Method: 引入UGC作为路径解析度量，其局部增量控制KL离散化误差。在log-reveal-odds坐标系中构造优化的单块与多块调度策略。UGC增量通过耦合揭示轨迹上的KL增量从样本中估计，从而构建certified-optimal采样器（以高概率达到指定KL误差，迭代复杂度在常数因子内逼近oracle）。在细划分极限下平方根UGC密度的平方积分决定最优Euler离散化误差的领头阶。

Result: 统一分析了Bernoulli子集和固定基数两类解掩码方案；证明了UGC与经典多元依赖度量及先前离散扩散复杂度度量之间的联系；在fine-partition极限下推导出尖锐的领头阶最优离散化误差公式；示例表明自适应块调度相比粗粒度调度可获得维度相关的显著提升，包括常数个自适应块就能实现~Ω(√d)量级的改进。

Conclusion: UGC为理解离散掩码扩散的采样复杂性提供了统一的路径解析框架，既连接了古典多元依赖理论，又催生了可认证最优的自适应采样算法，在理论上刻画了数据几何驱动的计算加速上限。

Abstract: We study masking diffusion for discrete sampling and introduce a path-resolved measure of data geometry called the \emph{unmasking growth complexity} ({\textsf{UGC}\xspace}). Its local increments directly control Kullback--Leibler (KL) discretization error, yielding a unified analysis of Bernoulli-subset and fixed-cardinality unmasking schemes. In log-reveal-odds coordinates, this structure yields optimized single-block and multi-block schedules, and quantifies the gains from adapting computational effort to data geometry. Crucially, we show how {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} increments can be estimated from samples via KL increments along coupled reveal trajectories. This leads to \emph{certified-optimal} samplers that achieve a prescribed KL error with high probability and iteration complexity within a constant factor of the corresponding oracle procedure. Collapsing the \ugc path yields the aggregate {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} mass, which connects to classical multivariate dependence measures and complexity measures from previous analyses of discrete diffusion. In the fine-partition limit, the squared integral of the square-root {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} density determines the sharp leading-order optimal Euler discretization error. Examples exhibit substantial dimension-dependent gains over coarse schedules, including $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{d})$ improvements achievable with a constant number of adaptively placed blocks.

</details>


### [252] [Vero: Can AI Agents Build Formally Verified Software Repositories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13522)
*Zhe Ye,Hantao Lou,Yuechun Sun,Peiyang Song,Zhengxu Yan,Timothe Kasriel,Qingyang Zhang,Kaiyu Yang,Soonho Kong,Jingxuan He,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Vero是首个在仓库级别评估联合实现与证明合成的基准，包含43个多模块实例（Python、Dafny、Verus、Coq），最强的前沿智能体仅能完全解决27/43个实例，表明当前智能体在仓库规模的验证软件合成上仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的验证代码生成基准要么只关注单个函数，要么只评估在给定实现下的证明生成，无法回答智能体能否在真实多模块代码库中做出连贯的实现与证明选择这一开放问题。为了保证AI生成软件的可信性，需要在仓库级别同时评估实现与证明的合成能力。

Method: Vero包含从真实仓库中提取的43个多模块Lean 4实例，覆盖Python、Dafny、Verus、Coq等语言来源及密码协议、分布式系统等领域。每个实例包含预定义API接口、人工整理的规范、参考实现，支持仅证明和代码+证明两种评估模式。同时引入审计机制，允许智能体正式证明给定规范不可满足或参考代码不正确，以发现并纠正策展过程中的潜在代码与规范错误。

Result: 在评估配备Lean工具链的前沿编码智能体配置时，最强智能体仅完全解决了43个实例中的27个，且在最难的仓库上没有任何规范被关闭，说明当前智能体在仓库级验证软件合成任务上仍显著欠缺。

Conclusion: Vero为衡量仓库规模验证软件合成的进展提供了具体测试平台，揭示了当前智能体在该任务上的不足，并通过开源基准、策展流程和评估工具推动该方向的发展。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used for programming, but do not provide any guarantee on the correctness of generated code. Verified code generation, in which an agent produces both an implementation and a machine-checked proof of its specification, offers a stronger path toward trustworthy AI-generated software. Existing benchmarks in this direction either focus on individual functions or only evaluate proof generation with provided implementations. It is still an open question whether agents can make coherent implementation and proof choices across real multi-module codebases. To bridge this gap, we introduce Vero, the first benchmark to evaluate joint implementation and proof synthesis at the repository level. Vero contains 43 multi-module instances sourced from real-world repositories spanning Python, Dafny, Verus, and Coq, and covering diverse domains from cryptographic protocols to distributed systems. Each instance consists of a multi-module Lean 4 repository with predetermined API interfaces, manually curated formal specifications, and reference implementations, supporting both proof-only and code-and-proof evaluation modes. To improve benchmark reliability, Vero also includes an audit mechanism where agents are allowed to formally prove unsatisfiability of provided specification or incorrectness of reference code, which surfaces and corrects latent code and specification errors during curation. We evaluate frontier coding-agent configurations with Lean toolchain access. The strongest agent fully solves only 27 of 43 instances and closes no specifications on the hardest repositories. Vero provides a concrete testbed for measuring progress toward repository-scale verified software synthesis, where current agents still fall short. We release the benchmark, curation pipeline, and evaluation harness at https://github.com/sunblaze-ucb/vero.

</details>


### [253] [DARTree: Speculative Diffusion Decoding with Autoregressive Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13524)
*Tianyi Li,Yaxin Luo,Xinyi Shang,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARTree是一种无需训练的投机解码方法，将预训练的AR纠错头从链式扩展为树式结构，通过批量扩展和评分构建固定宽度候选树，再使用最佳优先剪枝选择验证树。在七个基准测试中，DARTree在所有四种模型-温度配置下均取得最高平均接受长度和加速比，最高每轮接受12.97个token，相比DFlash提升98.6%，相比Domino提升27.9%，实现最高9.73倍的无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码通过并行验证多个草稿token来无损加速自回归语言模型。基于扩散的草稿器可并行预测整个token块以降低提案延迟，但其位置分布是边际分布而非条件于草稿路径上已选token的分布。现有循环纠错只沿单条草稿链携带因果信息，而基于扩散的树状构建虽拓宽了候选覆盖范围却未沿各分支进行纠错。本文旨在解决如何将循环纠错从链推广到树，同时避免顺序堆操作的推理开销。

Method: DARTree方法包括两个核心步骤：(1) 在每一深度批量扩展和评分所有节点，构建固定宽度的候选树，将AR纠错头的推理与顺序堆操作解耦；(2) 仅应用最佳优先剪枝来筛选验证树，从而在树结构中引入因果纠错信息。

Result: 在数学、代码和聊天共七个基准测试中，DARTree在所有四种模型-温度配置下均达到最高平均接受长度和加速比。每轮验证最多接受12.97个token，比DFlash高98.6%，比Domino高27.9%，相对本地测量的自回归解码实现最高9.73倍无损加速。

Conclusion: DARTree通过将预训练AR纠错头从链式扩展到树式结构，同时保留了扩散式树状构建的候选覆盖优势和循环纠错的因果信息，实现了更优的投机解码性能。其批量化节点扩展与最佳优先剪枝的设计有效解耦了AR头推理与顺序堆操作，在多个基准和配置下均取得最佳效果。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates autoregressive language models by verifying multiple draft tokens in parallel. Diffusion-based drafters further reduce proposal latency by predicting an entire token block in parallel, but their position-wise distributions are marginal rather than conditioned on tokens selected along each draft path. Existing recurrent correction incorporates causal information along a single draft chain, whereas diffusion-based tree construction broadens candidate coverage without carrying this correction along individual branches. We introduce DARTree, a training-free speculative decoding method that extends a pretrained AR correction head from chains to trees. DARTree first constructs a fixed-width candidate tree by expanding and scoring all nodes at each depth in a single batch, and then only applies best-first pruning to select the verification tree, decoupling AR-head inference from sequential heap operations. Across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, DARTree achieves the highest average acceptance length and speedup in all four model--temperature configurations, accepting up to 12.97 tokens per verification round, 98.6\% more than DFlash and 27.9\% more than Domino in the same setting, and reaching up to 9.73$\times$ lossless speedup over locally measured autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [254] [Exponential Convex Calibration Dimension for the Multi-Label Jaccard Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13549)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格分析了多标签分类与二值分割中Jaccard分数（IoU）的校准理论。证明Jaccard损失矩阵的非奇异性与仿射维度，给出精确校准的VC维上下界（2^{s-1}到2^s-1），证明精确校准凸替代损失需要指数级预测坐标，但通过MinHash方法可实现多项式维度的近似校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jaccard分数（IoU）是多标签分类和二值分割的标准评价指标，但其理论性质（如损失矩阵的代数结构、精确校准所需的参数复杂度）尚未被系统研究。理解这些性质对于设计有效的替代损失函数和预测规则至关重要。

Method: 采用有限MinHash Gram表示与布尔Möbius反演相结合的方法证明损失矩阵的非奇异性；通过因子加权分布构造下界；提出F_1-to-Jaccard损失转移和MinHash平方损失替代函数以实现多项式维度的近似校准。

Result: 证明了Jaccard、平移损失和普通损失矩阵的非奇异性，损失列的仿射维度为2^s-1；精确校准的CC维满足2^{s-1}≤CCdim≤2^s-1；F_1替代函数可实现渐近Jaccard后悔界3-2√2；MinHash平方损失可实现任意α后悔槽的均匀保证，无符号变体维度为O((s^2+s log(1/ρ))/α^2)，有符号变体为O((s+log(1/ρ))/α^2)。

Conclusion: 零后悔的精确校准需要指数级维度，而任意固定可加后悔容差都可通过多项式维度实现。这揭示了Jaccard校准中指数与多项式复杂度之间的根本界限。

Abstract: The per-instance Jaccard score, or intersection over union (IoU), is standard in multi-label classification and binary segmentation. With $s$ labels, its loss matrix has $2^s$ outcomes and reports. Under the convention $\mathrm{Jac}(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, we prove that the Jaccard score, shifted-loss, and ordinary loss matrices are nonsingular and that the loss columns have affine dimension $2^s-1$. The proof combines a finite MinHash Gram representation with Boolean Möbius inversion. For exact calibration, we prove $2^{s-1} \leq \mathrm{CCdim}(L^{\mathrm{Jac}}) \leq 2^s-1$. The lower bound uses a factorially weighted distribution with $2^{s-1}+1$ supported outcomes and Bayes-optimal reports. Consequently, every exactly calibrated convex surrogate requires exponentially many prediction coordinates. We also give two polynomial-dimensional approximation guarantees with explicit regret transfers. A new $F_1$-to-Jaccard transfer turns an existing $(s^2+1)$-dimensional $F_1$ surrogate into a polynomial-time rule with asymptotic Jaccard regret at most $3-2\sqrt{2}$. For any $α>0$ and $0<ρ<1$, a MinHash square-loss surrogate attains Jaccard-regret floor $α$ uniformly over arbitrary conditional label distributions. With probability at least $1-ρ$, the direct construction has dimension $O((s^2+s\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$, while a signed variant has dimension $O((s+\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$. Thus zero-regret calibration requires exponential dimension, whereas every fixed additive regret tolerance admits polynomial prediction dimension.

</details>


### [255] [Defensive Boosting for Online Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13554)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Defensive Booster"算法，将在线梯度提升（Brier分数竞争性）和在线弱到强分类提升（弱学习条件下的分类误差趋零）两种不相容的保证统一到单一高效框架中，仅需访问一个弱假设类学习器，并通过"对偶视角"将高分类误差转化为弱学习条件失败的硬核证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线提升技术只能分别提供两种互补但不兼容的保证：在线梯度提升使Brier分数与H的跨度竞争，但在跨度缺乏准确预测器时无任何保障；在线弱到强提升在弱学习条件下将分类误差驱至零，但在条件不满足时几乎无保证。需要一个统一框架同时获得两种保证。

Method: 提出防御性提升（Defensive Booster）算法，通过操作化提升的"对偶视角"实现统一保证：当随机化分类误差持续偏高时，算法维护的mistake weights形成平滑的重加权分布，使所有弱假设在其上的edge都很低，从而提供弱学习条件失效的ex-post硬核证书。算法仅访问单个弱类学习器（而非此前方法所需的大量弱学习器集成），并进一步提出强自适应变体，在任意时间区间上同时满足两种保证。

Result: 理论层面，该算法在对自适应序列上的Brier分数竞争性达到与在线梯度提升相同的速率，同时在满足光滑弱学习条件的转录上，Brier分数与随机化分类误差达到与在线分类提升相同的速率保证。实验层面，在合成与真实数据流上表现出强大的预测性能，有时显著超越所有先前基线，同时运行速度快数个数量级。

Conclusion: Defensive Booster以极简的架构（仅一个弱学习器）同时实现两种先前分离的在线提升保证，并提供了强自适应变体以满足任意时间区间上的双重保证，兼具理论完备性与实际高效性。

Abstract: We study online probabilistic forecasting of binary outcomes chosen by an adaptive adversary. Given an online learning algorithm for a weak hypothesis class $H$, we would like to efficiently obtain two incomparable guarantees that existing online boosting techniques provide separately. Online gradient boosting competes in Brier score with the best predictor induced by the span of $H$ on every sequence, but promises nothing when the span does not contain an accurate predictor. Online weak-to-strong boosting drives classification error to zero under a weak-learning condition, but promises little when that condition fails.
  We give a simple defensive forecasting algorithm, the Defensive Booster, that obtains both guarantees. On every adaptive sequence, its Brier score is competitive with the best prediction induced by the span of $H$ at the same rate as online gradient boosting; simultaneously, whenever the realized transcript satisfies the smooth weak-learning condition, its Brier score and randomized classification error satisfy the same rate guarantee as online classification boosting. This is achieved by operationalizing the "dual view" of boosting: When the algorithm's randomized classification error is persistently high, its mistake weights form a smooth reweighting on which every weak hypothesis has low edge, yielding an ex-post hard-core certificate that the weak-learning condition fails. We also develop a strongly adaptive variant, which satisfies both guarantees on every time interval. The Defensive Booster is very efficient: it accesses just one weak-class learner, whereas the prior online boosting methods we compare against maintain large weak-learner ensembles. Experiments on synthetic and real data streams demonstrate its strong predictive performance (sometimes substantially improving over all prior baselines) coupled with orders-of-magnitude faster runtime.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [RoboSynChallenge: Mastering Real-World Dexterity via Generalizing Synthesized Manipulation Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12416)
*Runyi Zhao,Ruixin Wu,Chengkun Li,Hongrui Zhang,Ang Li,Ruixing Jin,Yueci Deng,Yingying Guo,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Tianfan Xue,Yanjun Gao,Yudong Luo,Pascal Poupart,Simo Wu,Kui Jia,Wei-shi Zheng,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSynChallenge竞赛提出了一个统一的基准，结合大规模合成数据生成与标准化真实世界机器人评估，以提升操作策略的泛化能力。提供包括Transformer、扩散模型、VLA和世界-动作模型在内的基线实现，最终评估仅在未见的真实操作环境中进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能中机器人操作泛化仍具挑战，真实世界数据稀缺且多样性不足，限制了模型架构和算法的进展。

Method: 通过RoboSynChallenge竞赛引入统一基准，整合大规模合成数据生成与标准化真实机器人评估；鼓励参与者利用合成状态-动作轨迹提升通用策略学习，最终仅在未见真实环境中评估；提供多种基线实现（Transformer、扩散、VLA、世界-动作模型）以保证可复现性与可比性。

Result: 提出了将可扩展的仿真训练与严格真实世界验证相结合的竞赛框架，覆盖多任务、多环境、多难度水平，旨在培养广泛能力、数据高效且自适应的操作系统。

Conclusion: RoboSynChallenge通过仿真-真实耦合的评估体系，为通用机器人智能的发展提供了统一的推进路径。

Abstract: Achieving generalizable robotic manipulation remains a central challenge in embodied intelligence. Despite rapid advances in model architectures and learning algorithms, progress is often limited by the scarcity and narrow diversity of real-world data. The RoboSynChallenge competition introduces a unified benchmark to evaluate and advance the generalizability of manipulation policies across a spectrum of tasks, environments, and difficulty levels. To alleviate the shortage of realistic data, the challenge integrates large-scale synthetic data generation with standardized real-world robotic evaluation. Participants are encouraged to leverage synthesized state-action trials to improve general-purpose policy learning, while final assessments are conducted exclusively on unseen real-world manipulation environments. Baseline implementations, including Transformer-, Diffusion-, Vision-Language-Action, and World-Action-Model-based policies, are provided to ensure reproducibility and comparability. By coupling scalable simulation-based training with rigorous real-world validation, RoboSynChallenge aims to foster the development of broadly capable, data-efficient, and adaptable manipulation systems, thereby paving the way toward truly general robotic intelligence.

</details>


### [257] [Attune: A Self-Annotation Tool for Understanding Robot Operator Attention Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12650)
*Puqi Zhou,Sungsoo Ray Hong,David Porfirio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Attune的预部署引导工具，利用操作员眼动追踪作为机器人行为设计的线索，帮助多机器人监督系统的设计者理解和校准机器人行为以捕获、维持或释放操作员的注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人监督场景中，人类操作员需同时监督多个机器人，管理操作员注意力是界面设计的基本挑战。现有研究缺乏关于如何通过改变机器人行为来捕获、维持或释放操作员注意力的经验指导，设计师需要工具来理解操作员的注意力模式。

Method: 将操作员眼动凝视作为机器人行为设计的线索，构建了名为Attune的预部署引导工具。Attune自动识别有意义的凝视转移，提供AI辅助注释凝视转移原因，并输出操作员凝视模式摘要供其审阅。通过用户研究让参与者标注吸引其注意力的视觉触发因素来评估该工具。

Result: 研究发现用户观察到的凝视模式存在个体差异，Attune能够有效地帮助刻画和表征操作员的注意力特征。

Conclusion: Attune为多机器人监督界面的设计者提供了一种新的方法论工具，使其能够基于操作员注意力特征来校准机器人行为，填补了该领域在行为层面缺乏实证指导的空白。

Abstract: Deploying robot fleets in complex, real-world environments requires human operators to supervise multiple robots simultaneously. Managing operator attention is a fundamental challenge of designing multi-robot supervision interfaces, encompassing both feed layout and feed content (i.e., robot behavior design). Thus far, designers lack empirical guidance on the latter-how to change a robot's behavior to capture, sustain, or relinquish operator attention during multi-robot supervision. In our vision of the future, designers should be able to use this guidance to calibrate robot behavior to different operator attention profiles. Treating operator eye gaze as a robot behavior design clue, we created a pre-deployment elicitation tool called Attune. Attune automatically identifies when meaningful gaze shifts occur, provides AI assistance for annotating why shifts occurred, and outputs a summary of operator gaze patterns for operator review. We evaluated Attune through a user study in which participants annotated the visual triggers that drew their attention. Our findings unveil variation in observed gaze patterns and reveal how Attune helps characterize operator attention.

</details>


### [258] [FUSE: Active Functional Affordance Grounding through Adaptive Semantic-Geometric Evidence Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12683)
*Zhou Chen,Sathyanarayanan N. Aakur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出主动功能可供性定位（Active Functional Affordance Grounding）任务，并引入FUSE框架——一种结合显式不确定性驱动探索与学习型摊销规划器的自适应语义-几何证据采集方法，用于在代理主动探索场景时基于功能查询定位物体。实验表明FUSE在非Oracle定位性能上最优，且计算量较完全显式探索降低1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可供性（affordance）定位方法仅从固定视角工作，缺乏在功能线索被遮挡或不完整时决定"看向哪里"的机制。然而具体化代理（embodied agents）常需根据对象的功能而非身份来识别和交互，这要求代理主动采集能够揭示判别性功能证据的观测。

Method: 提出了FUSE（adaptive semantic-geometric evidence acquisition framework），其核心包含两部分：(1) 显式不确定性驱动的探索机制，用于识别信息不充分的区域；(2) 学习型摊销规划器（learned amortized planner），用于高效选择信息增益最大的视角。此外，作者还构建了基于Habitat的基准测试环境来评估主动功能接地任务。

Result: 实验结果显示FUSE在非Oracle定位性能上达到最优（最高观测性能），同时相比完全显式探索方法将计算量减少了1.33倍，并且在多种可供性知识源下保持有效性。

Conclusion: FUSE有效解决了主动功能可供性定位问题，通过结合显式与摊销策略实现了性能与计算效率之间的良好权衡，证明了不确定性驱动探索与学习型规划相结合在处理功能证据不完整场景时的可行性。

Abstract: Embodied agents must often identify and interact with objects based on their function rather than their identity, requiring them to actively acquire observations that reveal discriminative functional evidence. Existing affordance grounding methods operate from fixed viewpoints and lack mechanisms for deciding where to look when functional cues are occluded or incomplete. We introduce Active Functional Affordance Grounding, a new task in which an agent sequentially explores a scene to identify and spatially ground an object satisfying a functional query. To address this problem, we propose FUSE, an adaptive semantic-geometric evidence acquisition framework that combines explicit uncertainty-driven exploration with a learned amortized planner to efficiently select informative viewpoints. We further introduce a Habitat-based benchmark for evaluating active functional grounding. Experiments show that FUSE achieves the highest observed non-oracle grounding performance while reducing computation by 1.33x relative to fully explicit exploration, and remains effective across multiple affordance knowledge sources.

</details>


### [259] [SAP-Nav: Spatial Semantic Representation Meets Active Perception for Hierarchical Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12707)
*Xuetong Pei,Jian Liu,Vidura Munasinghe,Bo Miao,U-Xuan Tan,Wenrui Ding,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAP-Nav是一个全在线、零样本的层次化开放词汇目标导航框架，通过主动感知同时解决空间语义定位和目标验证的需求，在LangMap和HM3D-OVON基准上取得最佳性能，并在真实机器人实验中验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次化开放词汇目标导航（OVON）要求智能体在未见环境中根据自由形式指令（可能包含场景级、房间级、区域级和实例级线索）定位目标。虽然已有的LangMap形式化了这一设定，但在部分观测条件下可靠地解决该问题仍具挑战：空间定位需要持久的场景级证据积累，而目标验证需要清晰且具有区分度的候选视角。现有方法无法同时满足这两个需求。

Method: SAP-Nav提出两个核心组件：(1) 可查询空间语义表示（Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation），通过主动获取的房间视图增量构建，支持从任意已探索位置进行空间语义查询；(2) 主动视角验证（Active Viewpoint Verification），评估当前观测是否提供足够证据，若不足则重新定位智能体到信息更丰富的视角，再根据类别和属性约束验证候选目标。该方法完全在线运行，无需任务特定训练或预计算场景地图。

Result: 在LangMap和HM3D-OVON基准上，SAP-Nav取得了整体最优性能，其中在区域级导航任务上相比基于训练的方法SR提升了12.2%。真实机器人实验进一步验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: SAP-Nav通过主动感知机制有效解决了层次化OVON中空间语义定位与目标验证之间的矛盾，既支持层次化导航也支持标准类别级OVON，且无需预训练和预计算场景地图，展现出良好的泛化能力和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Hierarchical open-vocabulary object navigation (OVON) requires agents to follow free-form instructions that may specify targets through scene-, room-, region-, and instance-level cues in unseen environments. Although recent work LangMap has formalized this setting, reliably solving it under partial observations remains challenging: spatial grounding requires persistent environment-level evidence, whereas target verification requires clear and discriminative candidate views. We present SAP-Nav, a fully online, zero-shot framework that addresses both requirements through active perception. SAP-Nav incrementally constructs a Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation from actively acquired room views, enabling spatial semantic queries from any explored location. It further employs Active Viewpoint Verification to assess whether the current observation provides sufficient evidence and, when necessary, reposition the agent to a more informative viewpoint before verifying candidates against category and attribute constraints. Although designed for hierarchical OVON, SAP-Nav supports both hierarchical and standard category-level OVON without task-specific training or precomputed scene maps. Experiments on LangMap and HM3D-OVON show that SAP-Nav achieves the overall best performance, including a 12.2% improvement in SR over training-based methods on region-level navigation. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate its practical feasibility. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [260] [Genetic Fuzzy System-Based Multi-Robot Coordination for Planetary Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12755)
*Daegyun Choi,Donghoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于遗传模糊系统的多机器人系统分散式协作搬运方法，通过地形可通过性分析将高程图转换为二维可通行地图，利用遗传算法优化的模糊推理系统生成机器人速度指令，在非结构化环境中最小化搬运总路径长度并避开障碍物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作搬运物体在非结构化环境中面临路径规划和避障的挑战，现有集中式方法计算复杂度高且鲁棒性不足，需要一种分散式且能适应复杂地形环境的解决方案来最小化搬运总路径长度。

Method: 采用遗传模糊系统：首先基于高程图进行坡度相关的可通行性分析，将不可通行区域视为障碍物并转换为二维可通行地图；然后设计模糊推理系统（FIS）生成搬运物体的机器人速度，通过遗传算法在多种场景（局部极小值、目标靠近障碍物、杂乱环境）下优化FIS参数。

Result: 训练后的FIS模型在转换为可通行地图的测试环境中进行了验证，并通过多个测试场景证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: 遗传模糊系统能够有效实现多机器人系统的分散式协作搬运任务，在非结构化环境中实现路径长度最小化和避障目标。

Abstract: This paper proposes a decentralized approach for a multi-robot system (MRS) using a genetic fuzzy system to perform a collaborative object transportation task that minimizes the total path length of the MRS in unstructured environment while avoiding obstacles. For an environment given by an elevation map, terrain traversability analysis with respect to the slope is performed to reduce the dimension and identify non-traversable areas that can be considered as obstacles, and the given map is converted into a traversability map in two dimensional space. In the training process, proposed fuzzy inference systems (FISs) to generate the MRS's velocity for transporting an object to a target position are optimized by a genetic algorithm with several scenarios, such as a local minima, a target that is close to an obstacle, and a cluttered environment. The trained FIS models are applied to the testing environment, which is the converted traversability map, and validated using multiple scenarios.

</details>


### [261] [AirForesight: Current-to-Future Spatial Map Imagination with Cross-Space Planning Consistency for UAV-VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12835)
*Yutong Liu,Xiaojie Li,Mingzhu Xu,Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AirForesight框架，一种“当前到未来”的空间地图想象方法，用于无人机视觉-语言导航（UAV-VLN）。通过结构化当前地图表示、未来轨迹预测监督和跨空间规划一致性损失，从稀疏多视角观测中推断空间结构并执行3D运动。在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN方法大多直接将视觉-语言输入映射到动作，缺乏显式的场景接地（scene grounding）和未来感知的空间推理能力，导致在复杂户外环境中导航性能受限。

Method: 1) 从多视角观测学习结构化当前地图表示，并用当前地图重建和未来轨迹预测双重监督；2) 在结构化因果注意力下将当前空间知识传播到未来地图推理；3) 聚合当前与未来表示以预测下一个3D路点；4) 引入跨空间规划一致性损失，使预测的地图空间轨迹与基于真值路点位移的专家动作方向对齐。

Result: 在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S基准上取得强性能，广泛的消融实验验证了框架的有效性和稳定性。

Conclusion: AirForesight通过显式的当前到未来空间地图想象，为UAV-VLN提供了更强的场景接地和未来感知能力，验证了空间表示学习与规划一致性约束在无人机导航中的有效性。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Vision-Language Navigation (UAV-VLN) requires agents to follow language instructions, infer spatial structure from sparse multi-view observations, and execute feasible 3D motion in complex outdoor environments. Despite recent progress with large language models, most existing methods still map vision-language inputs directly to actions, providing limited explicit scene grounding and future-aware spatial reasoning. We propose AirForesight, a current-to-future spatial map imagination framework for UAV-VLN. AirForesight first learns a structured current-map representation from multi-view observations. This representation is jointly supervised by current-map reconstruction and future-trajectory prediction, encouraging it to encode both present scene structure and future motion intent. Under structured causal attention, the current spatial knowledge is propagated to future-map reasoning, and the resulting current and future representations are aggregated to predict the next 3D waypoint. To make spatial imagination more relevant to navigation, we introduce a cross-space planning consistency loss that encourages directional agreement between the predicted map-space trajectory and the expert action direction derived from the ground-truth waypoint displacement. Experiments on OpenUAV and AerialVLN-S, together with extensive ablations, demonstrate strong performance and support the effectiveness and stability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [262] [ASPIRE-VINS: Adaptive Spline-based Visual-inertial Navigation System With Robust 3D Measurement Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12840)
*Kwangyik Jung,Eungchang Mason Lee,Taekjun Oh,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ASPIRE-VINS是一个结合自适应节点放置（AKP）、多分辨率样条（MRS）和3D测量空间残差（3D-MSR）的连续时间视觉惯性导航系统框架，通过运动自适应地分配样条节点并在切线空间添加有界局部细化，以处理快速动态和静态区间的不同时间分辨率需求，在实验中取得了优于或匹敌基线方法的轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代离散时间VINS方法虽然精度和效率优异，但基于关键帧的表示在残差需要在任意时间戳评估或需要运动相关的时间分辨率时不够灵活。连续时间样条虽有改进，但均匀分布的节点在处理快速动态时表征不足，在静态区间又会过度参数化。

Method: 提出了ASPIRE-VINS框架，包含三个核心组件：1）自适应节点放置（AKP）根据局部运动变化分配样条节点；2）多分辨率样条（MRS）在切线空间中添加有界局部细化；3）3D测量空间残差（3D-MSR）通过将变换后的特征与3D测量空间中的校准观测射线对齐来提供方位一致性约束。

Result: 实验表明ASPIRE-VINS在各种运动和感知条件下实现了相对较低或具有竞争力的轨迹误差，验证了运动自适应连续时间轨迹建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 运动自适应的连续时间样条表示能够有效兼顾快速动态的高时间分辨率需求和静态区间的高效参数化，为连续时间VINS轨迹建模提供了一种灵活且准确的方案。

Abstract: Visual-inertial navigation systems estimate six-degree-of-freedom motion by fusing visual and inertial data. Modern discrete-time methods with IMU preintegration provide strong accuracy and efficiency, but keyframe-based representations can be less flexible when residuals must be evaluated at arbitrary timestamps or when motion-dependent temporal resolution is needed. Continuous-time splines address this issue by representing the trajectory as a smooth temporal function, but uniformly spaced knots can under-represent rapid dynamics or over-parameterize static intervals. This letter proposes ASPIRE-VINS, a continuous-time VINS framework that combines adaptive knot placement (AKP), multi-resolution splines (MRS), and 3D measurement-space residuals (3D-MSR). AKP allocates knots according to local motion variation, MRS adds bounded local refinement in tangent space, and 3D-MSR provides bearing consistency by aligning transformed features with calibrated observation rays in 3D measurement space. Experiments show that ASPIRE-VINS achieves competitive or lower trajectory errors than the compared baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of motion-adaptive continuous-time trajectory modeling under diverse motion and sensing conditions.

</details>


### [263] [BrainWAM: Action-Space Coordination of Semantic Priors and Predictive Dynamics for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12854)
*Bing Zhan,Shuyao Shang,Jiahao Gu,Shuo Lu,Yuan Xu,Zhao Wang,Yida Wang,Xueyang Zhang,Kun Zhan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrainWAM是一个结构化的动作空间协调框架，将语义推理与预测性世界建模整合为两条专业化的动作导向通路，在紧凑动作表示层面进行对齐，并引入异步整流流推断策略，在NAVSIM v1和v2基准上达到SOTA性能（89.5 PDMS / 89.6 EPDMS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要在语义约束和预测动力学两方面进行规划，但现有端到端方法（VLA模型和世界动作模型WAMs）通常只侧重其中一方面。不过，简单的联合token级注意力组合会导致注意力分配失配问题——语义捷径主导共享注意力空间并抑制预测动力学，因此需要一种统一的规划框架来同时利用两方面优势。

Method: 受神经科学中复杂行为源于功能特化系统协调的启发，BrainWAM将语义推理和预测世界建模转化为两条专业化的动作导向通路，并在紧凑动作表征层面进行对齐。同时提出异步整流流（asynchronous rectified-flow）推断策略，通过解耦视频与动作去噪过程来缩短推断延迟，同时保留与规划相关的预测上下文。

Result: BrainWAM在NAVSIM v1上达到89.5 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上达到89.6 EPDMS，均达到SOTA，持续优于仅使用VLA或仅使用WAM的方法。

Conclusion: BrainWAM通过结构化动作空间协调框架有效融合了语义先验与预测动力学，避免了朴素组合策略中注意力分配失配的问题，为自动驾驶系统提供了一种实用且有前景的方向。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires planning under both semantic constraints and predictive dynamics. Existing end-to-end driving approaches, however, typically emphasize only one side of this requirement: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exploit VLM priors for semantic reasoning, while World Action Models (WAMs) provide future-aware prediction through generative world modeling. This naturally motivates a unified planner that can leverage both semantic priors and predictive dynamics. However, we find that a naive combination through joint token-level attention suffers from an attention-allocation mismatch, where semantic shortcuts dominate the shared attention space and suppress predictive dynamics. Inspired by neuroscience evidence that complex behavior arises from coordination among functionally specialized systems, we propose BrainWAM, a structured action-space coordination framework that converts semantic reasoning and predictive world modeling into two specialized action-oriented pathways, and aligns them at the level of compact action representations. We further introduce an asynchronous rectified-flow inference strategy with decoupled video and action denoising, which shortens inference latency while preserving planning-relevant predictive context. BrainWAM reaches state-of-the-art performance on both NAVSIM v1 (89.5 PDMS) and NAVSIM v2 (89.6 EPDMS), consistently outperforming VLA-only or WAM-only methods, highlighting BrainWAM as a practical and promising direction for autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [264] [HumanoidVLN: A Physics-Grounded Simulator and Benchmark for Vision-Language Navigation Across Diverse Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12860)
*Quan-Dung Pham,Anh Dao,The-Anh Nguyen,Minh Nguyen-Dinh,Phuong Nam Dang,Tri Pham,Hung Tran,Bach Dao,Tuyen P. Le,Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了HumanoidVLN，一个基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建的物理仿真VLN基准，支持四种不同构型的人形机器人（10-12个下肢自由度，身高1.17-1.80m），通过分层控制栈（RL运动策略+PD/MPC路径跟踪器）实现。包含933个碰撞感知参考episode及多风格指令，兼容NaVILA、DualVLN、StreamVLN和JanusVLN四种VLN模型。实验表明JanusVLN达到最高成功率43.55%和nDTW 48.38；20-episode sim-to-real实验中导航误差与仿真高度相关（r=0.935）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN基准未能解决人形机器人导航面临的独特挑战：双足运动引入的物理约束（轮式智能体不存在）、人形形态在不同平台间的差异性、以及运动导致的相机动态对自我中心观察的畸变影响。需要一个物理接地、多形态的仿真平台来填补这一空白。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建HumanoidVLN仿真平台，采用分层控制栈（RL运动策略与可替换的PD/MPC路径跟踪器）；支持四个机器人平台；环境来自艺术家设计场景和3D高斯泼溅重建（过滤导航面积>100m²）；借助生成器-审查器-改写器的多智能体pipeline及人工验证生成指令，每个episode配一条细粒度指令和三种风格变体。

Result: 四种模型×四种形态的实验中，JanusVLN取得最高平均成功率43.55%和nDTW 48.38。20-episode sim-to-real pilot中，导航误差相关系数r=0.935，平均绝对误差0.68m，平均轨迹相似度nDTW为0.782（±0.188）。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示了VLN模型、控制器与人形形态之间在物理执行条件下的交互关系。性能差距表明人形具身形态和运动控制器对VLN任务有显著影响，该基准可作为未来人形VLN研究的标准测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) for humanoid robots poses challenges existing benchmarks fail to address: bipedal locomotion imposes physical constraints absent from wheeled agents, humanoid morphologies vary across platforms, and egocentric observations are distorted by locomotion-induced camera dynamics. We present HumanoidVLN, a physics-grounded simulator and benchmark for VLN across diverse humanoid embodiments. Built on NVIDIA Isaac Sim, our platform supports an extensible set of humanoid configurations, demonstrated on four robots (Unitree G1, Unitree H1, Internal-A, Internal-B) spanning 10-12 lower-body DoF and heights from 1.17m to 1.80m, via a hierarchical control stack combining a reinforcement learning locomotion policy with interchangeable PD or MPC path trackers. New robots and VLN models integrate with minimal effort; we demonstrate compatibility with NaVILA, DualVLN, StreamVLN, and JanusVLN. Environments are drawn from artist-designed scenes and 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructions, filtered for navigable areas exceeding 100 square meters. Instructions are generated by a dual generator-reviewer plus paraphraser multi-agent pipeline with human-in-the-loop verification, yielding 933 collision-aware reference episodes, each paired with one fine-grained instruction and three coarse-grained stylistic variants (formal, natural, casual). Across four models and four embodiments, JanusVLN achieves the highest mean success rate of 43.55% and nDTW of 48.38. In a 20-episode sim-to-real pilot with DualVLN and the Unitree G1, navigation errors correlate strongly (r=0.935), with a mean absolute difference of 0.68m and mean trajectory similarity of 0.782 (+/-0.188) nDTW. These results highlight the interaction between VLN models, controllers, and humanoid embodiments under physical execution. Code, benchmark, and data will be released upon acceptance at https://humanoid-vln.github.io/.

</details>


### [265] [AMR-Pose: An Active LED Marker-Based Relative Pose Estimation Framework With Probabilistic Switching PnP for Cooperative AUVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12866)
*Zeyu Sha,Xiaorui Wang,Mingyang Yang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AMR-Pose，一种基于主动LED标记的自主水下航行器（AUV）相对位姿估计框架。采用一个红色中心LED加三个蓝色外围LED的紧凑标记模块，并结合概率性切换PnP估计器（PSwPnP），在SE(3)上进行李群位姿传播、概率标记关联和可见性自适应测量融合，实现六自由度相对位姿的鲁棒估计，并通过水槽实验和闭环领航-跟随实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV间的可靠相对位姿估计对协同海洋探索、采样和多机器人协调至关重要，但水下环境存在浊度、光照变化、反射和特征遮挡等严重光学退化问题，使得基于视觉的相对定位极具挑战性。

Method: 开发了一个由单个红色中心LED和三个蓝色外围LED组成的紧凑标记模块，集成于领航AUV上以提供独特视觉特征；在此基础上设计了概率性切换PnP估计器（PSwPnP），该估计器结合了SE(3)上的李群位姿传播、概率性标记关联以及可见性自适应测量融合，能够根据标记可见性动态调整估计过程，在部分观测和可见性切换时保持几何一致性和时间稳定性。

Result: 基于运动捕捉系统地面真值的水槽实验验证了AMR-Pose在挑战性水下条件下能够实现准确、平滑且鲁棒的相对位姿估计；闭环领航-跟随实验进一步证明了其在协同水下机器人中用于实时相对位姿反馈的可行性。

Conclusion: AMR-Pose作为一种主动LED标记驱动的相对位姿估计框架，有效解决了水下光学退化对视觉定位的干扰问题，为协同AUV系统提供了一种精确、稳定且实用的相对定位方案。

Abstract: Reliable relative pose estimation between autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) is critical for cooperative ocean exploration, sampling, and multi-robot coordination. However, achieving robust vision-based relative localization in underwater environments remains challenging due to severe optical degradation, including turbidity, illumination variations, reflections, and intermittent feature occlusions. This paper presents AMR-Pose, an active LED marker-based relative pose estimation framework for cooperative AUVs. A compact marker module consisting of one red central LED and three blue peripheral LEDs is developed and integrated onto the leader AUV to provide distinctive visual features under complex underwater conditions. Building upon the detected marker observations, a probabilistic switching Perspective-n-Point estimator (PSwPnP) is developed by combining Lie-group pose propagation on $SE(3)$, probabilistic marker association, and visibility-adaptive measurement fusion for robust six-degree-of-freedom relative pose estimation. The proposed framework dynamically adapts the estimation process according to marker visibility, maintaining geometric consistency and temporal stability during partial observations and visibility transitions. Extensive water-tank experiments with motion-capture ground truth validate that AMR-Pose achieves accurate, smooth, and robust relative pose estimation under challenging underwater conditions. Closed-loop leader-follower experiments further demonstrate its feasibility for real-time relative pose feedback in cooperative underwater robotics.

</details>


### [266] [Temporal GRPO: Beyond Trajectory-Level Credit in Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13026)
*Yao Zhou,Hang Gao,Fengge Wu,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出时间GRPO（Temporal GRPO），通过构建可检测的任务阶段、将轨迹对齐到阶段特定的动作区间，并仅比较进入相同阶段的 rollout，来解决基于GRPO的VLA后训练中轨迹级信用分配混叠问题，从而提升任务成功率和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在常见的基于GRPO的VLA后训练中，单一 rollout 级的优势值被应用到轨迹中的每个动作。若一个 rollout 完成了多个有效阶段但后续失败，其早期阶段产生的有效进步动作会被错误地惩罚，这种"轨迹级信用混叠"（trajectory-level credit aliasing）问题阻碍了策略的有效学习。

Method: Temporal GRPO 提出三个阶段化方案：(1) 构建可检测的任务阶段，(2) 将每个 rollout 与阶段特定的动作区间对齐，(3) 仅比较已进入相同阶段的 rollout 来计算阶段级优势值。这些阶段优势值随后在单次策略更新中应用到对应的动作区间。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0基准上，Temporal GRPO 在任务成功率和样本效率上均有提升，且在不同任务长度上表现一致的增益。在LIBERO-Long上的受控更新实验中，该方法保留了共享的先决阶段，并将改进集中在 rollout 结果首次出现分歧的阶段。

Conclusion: Temporal GRPO 通过细粒度的阶段级信用分配，显著缓解了 VLA 后训练中的信用混叠问题，验证了时间维度上分层信用分配的有效性，为基于稀疏奖励的 VLA 策略优化提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Outcome-driven reinforcement learning offers a scalable way to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) policies from sparse task-success feedback. In common GRPO-based VLA post-training, one rollout-level advantage is applied to every action in the trajectory. A rollout that completes several valid stages but fails later can therefore penalize the actions that produced its earlier progress. We call this trajectory-level credit aliasing. Temporal GRPO addresses this problem by constructing detectable task stages, aligning each rollout with stage-specific action intervals, and comparing only rollouts that have entered the same stage. The resulting stage advantages are applied to their corresponding intervals in a single policy update. On RoboTwin 2.0, Temporal GRPO improves task success and sample efficiency, with consistent gains across task horizons. Controlled updates on LIBERO-Long preserve shared prerequisite stages and concentrate improvement at the first stage where rollout outcomes diverge.

</details>


### [267] [H2R-Bench: Benchmarking Human-to-Robot Manipulation Video Generation in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13049)
*Dingyi Rong,Yue Shi,Chaofan Ma,Jiezhang Cao,Zongrui Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Yao Mu,Guangtao Zhai,Ning Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了H2R-Bench基准，用于评估跨具身（human-to-robot）操作视频生成模型的性能，该基准将人类演示视频转换为特定机器人具身条件下的操作视频，并通过五维指标系统评估当前视频世界模型在此任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模操作数据对机器人学习至关重要，但机器人示范数据收集昂贵且难以扩展；尽管人类第一视角视频蕴含丰富的操作经验，跨具身迁移（人手到机器人末端执行器）仍然极具挑战。新兴的视频世界模型有望从人类观测中合成以机器人为中心的操作视频，但其跨具身迁移能力尚未被系统探索，因此需要一个专门的基准来评估该能力。

Method: 构建H2R-Bench基准，每个实例包含人类演示视频、目标具身约束以及源数据锚定的标注（任务目标、动作事件、功能接触和物体响应）。基准通过五个维度评估生成视频：目标状态完成度、动作事件完成度、功能接触迁移、具身正确性和一般视频质量。作者评估了11个SOTA视频生成模型，覆盖6个操作类别和2种机器人具身。

Result: 实验表明，当前视频世界模型在人类到机器人操作迁移方面仍然能力有限，即使是领先模型也常在具身一致性、功能交互和任务执行方面失败。

Conclusion: H2R-Bench为系统评估视频世界模型能否弥合人类到机器人具身差距、将人类操作观测转化为机器人中心训练资源提供了诊断框架，并揭示了现有模型的不足，为未来跨具身视频生成研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Large-scale manipulation data is essential for robot learning, yet collecting robot demonstrations remains expensive and difficult to scale. Meanwhile, abundant egocentric human manipulation videos provide rich behavioral experiences, but transferring them across embodiments remains challenging due to differences between human hands and robotic end-effectors. Recent advances in video world models offer a promising pathway to synthesize robot-centric manipulation videos from human observations, while their cross-embodiment transfer capability remains largely unexplored. Therefore, we introduce H2R-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating cross-embodiment human-to-robot manipulation video generation, where models transform egocentric human demonstrations into robot manipulation videos under specified embodiments. Each benchmark instance contains a human demonstration video, target embodiment constraints, and source-grounded annotations covering task goals, action events, functional contacts, and object responses. H2R-Bench evaluates generated videos through five dimensions, including goal-state completion, action-event completion, functional contact transfer, embodiment correctness, and general video quality. We benchmark eleven state-of-the-art video generation models across six manipulation families and two robot embodiments. Our evaluation reveals that current video world models remain limited in human-to-robot manipulation transfer: even leading models often fail in embodiment consistency, functional interaction, and task execution. H2R-Bench provides a systematic diagnostic framework for evaluating whether video world models can bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap and convert human manipulation observations into robot-centric training resources.

</details>


### [268] [Semantic Radiance Fields as Simulators for Spatial Reasoning in Real-World Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13095)
*Nico Heider,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Katarzyna Wasielewska-Michniewska,Przemysław Hołda,Martin Schieck,Marcin Paprzycki,Maria Ganzha,Bogdan Franczyk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用语义辐射场（SRF）作为具身智能体空间推理的仿真器，SRF将预训练视觉模型的2D语义分割提升到3D辐射场中，统一编码几何、外观和逐类语义身份，支持从真实场景RGB图像重建并用于物理引擎驱动的仿真环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身智能体空间推理训练与评估环境存在矛盾：合成仿真器具有真实语义标注但缺乏视觉真实性，而基于真实场景重建的仿真器视觉真实但默认缺乏语义标注。需要一个既能保证几何保真度又能提供可查询语义的环境。

Method: 提出使用语义辐射场（SRF）作为仿真器。具体方法为：从带位姿的RGB图像重建场景，将预训练视觉模型的2D语义分割结果提升为3D辐射场，联合编码几何、外观与逐类语义身份，支持新颖视角合成、语义查询和自由空间查询。

Result: SRF能够从真实场景生成多样化环境，用于训练和评估空间推理模型。作为示例应用，作者构建了一个基于SRF驱动的果园苹果采摘任务仿真器，辐射场为物理引擎提供相机渲染、语义真值和占据查询。

Conclusion: 语义辐射场统一了真实感与语义标注的需求，为空间推理智能体提供了一种兼顾几何保真度和语义可查询性的仿真方案，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Training and evaluating spatial reasoning in embodied agents requires diverse environments that are both geometrically faithful and semantically queryable. Synthetic simulators offer ground truth semantics but sacrifice realism; simulators based on reconstructions of real-world environments have realistic appearance but lack ground truth semantics by default. We propose using Semantic Radiance Fields (SRF) as simulators for spatial reasoning agents. SRFs are a representation that unifies these requirements by lifting 2D semantic segmentations from pretrained vision models into a 3D radiance field that jointly encodes geometry, appearance, and per-class semantic identity. The resulting fields are reconstructed from posed RGB captures of real scenes and support novel-view synthesis, semantic and free-space queries within a single grounded representation. This enables the efficient generation of diverse real-world environments to train and evaluate spatial reasoning models. As an example application, we outline an SRF-driven simulator for an orchard apple-reaching task, in which the radiance field supplies camera rendering, semantic ground truth, and occupancy queries to a physics engine.

</details>


### [269] [S2-HWM: Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model for Long-Horizon Surgical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13103)
*Shuzhe Zhang,Xin Zhu,Yinling Qian,Qiong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出S2-HWM，一种稀疏事件结构化分层世界模型，通过学习原始隐轨迹中的稀疏事件证据来协调事件级管理器与原始步工作器，解决长视野手术机器人操作中奖励稀疏和交互变化不规则的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野手术机器人操作任务具有奖励稀疏、关键交互变化发生在不规则时间间隔的特点。现有世界模型方法通常在原始步分辨率上想象，无法显式建模变时长的任务进展；手动指定的阶段边界难以与状态依赖的交互转换对齐。

Method: S2-HWM由事件级管理器（manager）和原始步工作器（worker）组成分层架构。从原始隐轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据，用于调度管理器的目标更新；每个选定的隐目标条件化工作器的原始动作直至下次更新。事件证据还形成变时长片段，用于事件转换模型（ETM）预测下一边界随机状态、片段时长和累计片段奖励。链式事件级预测提供超出原始想象视野的变时长延续来训练管理器，而工作器保留原始步actor-critic学习。

Result: 在SurRoL平台的PegTransfer任务上，S2-HWM取得98.7%的成功率，比平面GAS DreamerV3基线高出22.7个百分点。

Conclusion: S2-HWM通过自动学习的事件边界将分层世界模型与稀疏事件结构相结合，有效解决了长视野手术操作中不规则交互变化带来的挑战，显著优于平坦基线。

Abstract: Long-horizon surgical robot manipulation is challenging because task rewards are sparse, while meaningful interaction changes occur at irregular intervals. Existing world-model agents typically imagine at primitive-step resolution, leaving variable-duration task progress implicit.
